### Page 6 (OCR, spell-checked: 5 words corrected)

```text
WAAO-BECK systems LTD;
GENERAL
M52OOA CONTROLLER MODULE
The MS2OOA is the central , microprocessor based, data processing unit for the ST series of Stereo Television consoles.
This controller performs numerous supervisory control functions within the console system_ It controls the mute logic facilities for the mixing channels, integrated channel machine control start/stop functions, and control of the console monitoring channels (M525 )
The MS2OOA integrates and con trols the following optional fea tures of the ST console series; the M52L programmer unit, M526 overload status assemb Iy , the M5202 series of external routing Sw itchers, etc Six dual 8 bit parallel multiplexed address/data ports are made available for feeding data in and out of the control system. Two data ports are available for discrete control inputs and outputs. Sixteen functions per port are made available. The inputs for these discrete functions are isolated through optocoup lers and the outputs are isolated with dry contact relay closures
The logic family of integrated circuits is known as the 74HC series which is the latest in high speed CMOS technology . The logic levels for the data ports for this family of integrated circuits and the data ports of the M52OOA are nom finally 3.5 volts or greater for a logic 1 level and 1 volt or Less for a logic 0 level ; this is for a 5 volt supply .
A ninth data port, which has access to the internal bus structure of the M52O0A , is made available to allow for system expansion and customization
AII data ports are on 37 pin D female receptacles, while the internal data bus port is accessed through a 37 pin D ' male connector
The M52OOA is packaged in a standard nineteen inch rack mount assemb Iy . occupy ing one rack unit of vertical space (1.75 inches) and is sixteen inches  deep .
The MS2OOA is powered by an external 12 volt unregulated power supply . The power to the controller is via a 15 pin D ' connector.
2-1
```

```text
WAAD-BeCK SysTeMB LTD.
OPERAT IONAL DESCRIPT ION AND SYSTEM INTERCONNECT
M52OOA CONTROLLER MODULE
The MS2OOA is the central control Ling unit for ST series of Stereo Television consoles .
Data interconnects to major sections of the console system are via multiconductor ribbon cables which term ina te on standard 37 pin D ' connectors This multi-interconnect technique is used to provide the maximum degree of isolation between various functions as wel as facilitate the ease of system serv ice and expansion
The data ports connect to the console system in the following way:
J1 (7000) : Reads tally data from input channels 1 to 12 . Sends control data to remotely control the channel and solo on/off functions (applicable with M524 Programmer option) .
J2 (7100) : Reads  tally data from input channels 13 to 24 Sends control data to remotely control the channel and solo on/off functions applicable with M524 Programmer option)
J3 (7200) : Reads tally data from input channels 25 to 36 (ST3642) Sends control data to remotely control the channel and solo on/off functions (app liable with M524 Programmer option)
J4 (7300) : Reads tally data from submaster channels 1 to 4 . Sends control data to remotely control the sub and solo on/off functions (app Iicable with M524 Programmer option) J5 (7400) Reads pushbutton control information from the M525 Monitor Modules (2-5T2442 , 4-5T3642) Sends tally information to the M525 Monitor Module.
J6 (7500) :
Reads pushbutton control information from the optional M524 Programmer_ Sends tally information I0 the optional M524 Programmer and M526 Overload and Fault Status Modules .
J7 (7600) :
Reads discrete control inputs (16 available) Refer to st console system manuals for input function details.
3-1
```

```text
MASTERISLAVE
Switch SW1 must be left in the up Master Posi tion for the controller to correctly operate and process the systems exter- nal data. If placed in the Slave position all data ports are placed in a high impedance tri-state mode, disabling the controller. This mode presently does not have application in the ST series of consoles.
```

### Page 18 (OCR, spell-checked: 7 words corrected)

```text
WAAD-BeCK SystemS LTD;
CIRcUiT DESCRIPTION
SA14-094 CONTROLLER ASSEMBLY
Microprocessor
The NSC8OO cmoS microprocessor utilizes the 280 instruction set. It is an 8 bit processor with 16 bit addressing capability (64K) The lower eight address Lines are multiplexed onto the 8 bit data bus
The clock frequency of the microprocessor i5 crystal controlled by a 3 mHz crystal
Power Fail and Reset
Comparator UI1 senses a drop in the 5 volt supply voltage driving the non maskable interrupt of the microprocessor at pin 21 This arrests the execution of instructions before the power is totally lost . The output of U11 also inhibits the RAM memory (uio) at this time
014, U15 and 016 make up a power fail control circuit which uses time to establish whether a power interruption has occured or whether a full power failure has occured and the ST series of console system should be shut down (all channels, subs and monitor- ing turned off) This circuitry is powered from the backup nickel cadmium battery
On the occurance of power interruption, counter U14 is enabled at Pin 11 (switches to a logic 0) and the oscillator U15 pins 8 and 9 starts the count. The counter outputs Label led A through F are available to set the power interrupt time period The typical times are 8 seconds, 16 seconds, 30 seconds, 1 minute, 2 minutes and 4 minutes As shipped from the factory the 4 minute jumper is in.
The counter output at pin 11 of 015 sets the power fail Latch U16 (pins 8 through 13) The Latch output drives the fail LED DSI and through 015 pin 4 the fourth input of data buffer 024
If the power interruption is less than the set period of time (4 minutes) then the system wiii be restored to its state prior to the power interruption. If it lasts longer than the set time period the system upon recovery of power, wili remain in a shut down state (ali channels, subs and mon itors turned off and any control functions released)
The power fail Latch (416) is reset or cleared under software control This is achieved by the addressing output at U28 pin 5 (address select 7BOOH )
1-1
```

### Page 20 (OCR, spell-checked: 3 words corrected)

```text
WAAD-BECK SysTems LTD_
WARD-BeCK SYSTEms LTD;
CIRCUIT DESCRIPTION
ciRcuiT DESCRIPT ION
SA14-094 CONTROLLER ASSEMBL Y
5A1L-0g4 CONTROLLER ASSEMBLY
Each data port appears on a 37 pin 'D' female connector at the rear of the M5200 series of controller .
Latch 427 along with the oscillator composed of three sections of 428 , R78 , R79 , C18 and C19, form a Rea Time clock which may be used for timing various software system functions. The status of this clock is fed to bit #0 of buffer u24 For present applications in the St series of Stereo Television consoles this function is not utilized
Interna 4 Bus Buffers
The optional bi-directional transceiver buffers 435 , 436 and 437 feed the internal 8 bit data bus, 12 bits of addressing as well as control Line functions NRD , NWR and address select 4800H to data connector P9 .
Power Supply Decoupl ing
The unregulated 10/12 volt dc supply is fed into the M52OOA via P1o SW1 is a 1 .5 amp breaker switch.
This data access is for expansion capabilities and provides a means of direct memory access into and out of the control system.
The cathode of polarity reversing diode CRS connects to the 5 volt regulator U38 and the constant current charging circuit (Q1 and 02 ) for the nickel cadmium battery B1
Presently this feature is not utilized in the ST series of Stereo Television consoles.
The output voltage from regulator 438 is set by R131 R132 and The regulated supply voltage should be between 5.0 and 5.5 Ro33s _ ts .
926k; is a time of day clock chip that drives the optional clock and timer displays_ The clock is crystal controlled by XT2 (32.768 kHz) The optional clock is under complete software control
L Zener diode CR6 is backup protection in the event regulator u38 shou- Id fail , placing 12 volts on the supply rail. Under normal conditions this zener diode is not conducting The regulated 5 volt supply voltage, when present feeds the IC' s which are powered by the backup battery through Schottky diode CR8 Blocking transistor 03 is reverse biased and off when the supply voltage is Present. Upon the occurance of a power failure Q3 wili turn on , provided the base is grounded through the battery backup strap on power connector P1O (pins 15 and 7) _ The 3.6 volt battery voltage wili be applied to IC's U1O , u14, 415, 016, 026 , 430 , 432 and 433 . Under these conditions CR8 now blocks the backup voltage from the remainder of the circui try (CR8 is now reverse biased) Under normal operating conditions battery B1 is under constant current trickle charge through Q1 and 02 . Battery BI will feed the above mentioned circuitry anytime the supply voltage is off and power plug P10 is not removed No damage wili occur if the ba ttery is allowed to comp letely discharge. Breaker switch Sw2 feeds the 5 volt supply voltage to the other assemblies within the MS2OOA
Status_Display
A seven segment readout display on the front of the M52OOA (part of the SA14-096 Subassembly ) provides an indication of the processor operation.
Latch u25 stores the data bits, which in turn determ ines the segments of the status display that are illuminated. As the system executes different routines of the operating system software various segments are iVuminated, giving somewhat of a visual indication of the system operation .
Section 11 , 12 and 13 of u29 performs an AND gate select function between address select 7Fooh and the NWR control output from the microprocessor
Miscellaneous
U24 is an 8 bit buffer which is available for feeding unitary status inputs into the system. Presently input bit #4 reads the status of the power fail Latch . This buffer is addressed by select code 7DOOH
1-4
1-5
```

### Page 30 (OCR, spell-checked: 1 words corrected)

```text
WAAO-BECK systems LTD:
CiRcuiT DESCRIPTION
SA14-096 DISPLAY ASSEMBLY
This assemb y mounts to the front panel of the m5200 series of controllers and supports the pertinent display and front panel switch functions of the controller
The green indicators DS2 and DS3 show when the 5 volt power is present on the SA14-094 motherboard assembly and applied to the daughterboard assembly (5A14-095 or 5A1L-123 )
The red LED DSI 4 the On Line indica tor , shows when the controller is in the Master mode and processing data. This indicator is dependent upon the setting of the Master /Slave switch SwI
The Master /Slave switch Sw1 must be left in the Master position to process external data If placed in the Slave position all data ports are transferred into the tri-state high impedance conditions and the microprocessor goes into a standby mode This requirement or feature is presently not utilized in the control system of the ST series of Stereo Television consoles
Momentary pushbutton switch SW2 is a hardware means of resetting the M5200 microprocessor
The seven segment status display U1 is a software development tool that has been incorporated in the controller operating system The individual segments are used to show the execution of various software routines. This gives a visual indication that the software is executing correctly . The status Latch output which drives the display decimal point, pulses the hardware watchdog circuit. If the software stops operating correctly the decimal point wili not be alternately turned off and on , resulting in the watchdog resetting the microprocessor approximately every two seconds.
1-1
```

```text
Momentary pushbutton switch SW2 is a hardware means of resetting the M5200 microprocessor
The seven segment status display U1 is a software development tool that has been incorporated in the controller operating system The individual segments are used to show the execution of various software routines. This gives a visual indication that the software is executing correctly . The status Iatch output which drives the display decimal point, pulses the hardware watchdog circuit. If the software stops operating correctly the decimal point wili not be alternately turned off and on , resulting in the watchdog resetting the microprocessor approximately every two seconds.
1-1
```

### Page 35 (OCR, spell-checked: 6 words corrected)

```text
WAAD-BECK SYSTEMS LTD_
CIRcUIT DESCR PTION
SA14-123 MACHINE CONTROL INTERFACE ASSEMBL Y
The SA14-123 expands the number of isolated data ports that the MS2OOA has available to the outside world as well as providing a number of unitary discrete inputs and outputs _
Data Ports
This assembly contains three additional buffered data ports similar to that described on the SA14-094 assembly The programmable per_ ipheral interface chip U1 and its associated components will be described.
AI addressing and data into and out of this device (U1 ) is via an 8 bit address/data bus Control to U1 is through NRD , NWR 9 ALE and an address select line (7300H in this case) A reset Line (RST) clears all registers within all interface ch ips.
The two 8 bit bus outputs of U1 are utilized as bi-directional address/data buses to feed and receive data external to the M52O0A controller_ These buses are isolated by the bi-directional trans- ceivers u3 and U4 . The terminating networks on the external side of u3 and U4 prov ide the necessary isolation and loading to opti mally feed the interconnecting ribbon cables Lo
The 6 bit data port from U1 (pins 1 , 2, 5, 37 , 38 and 39) are configured as Latched outputs only _ They serve as select and control lines to the external dev ices of the data port_ Le The lower 3 bits serve as NRD , NWR and NALE control Lines . The upper 3 bits are fed through 1 of 8 decoder U2, which prov ides 6 address select outputs_ For certain applications the upper address select Line is inter- changed with the NALE control Line therefore when NALE is required, only 5 address select lines are available) _ Buffer u5 isolates these control lines to the outside world
Data ports 7300H 1 7400H and 7500H all operate in a similar manner and appear on 37 pin D ' connectors at the rear of the MS2OOA Control ler .
Discrete Data Inputs
Data port 7600H provides 16 isolated control inputs Each input is isolated with an opto-coupler with both sides of each input floating and isolated_ The LED of each opto-coupler may derive its power from the external con trolling device where total isolation is req- wired) or from the integral 5 volt supply which is pins 18 and 19 of the 37 pin female D ' connector .
1-1
```

### Page 36 (OCR, spell-checked: 8 words corrected)

```text
WAAD-BECK SYSTEMS LTD_
WAAD-BECK Systems LTD_
CIRcUIT DESCRIPTION
CIRCUIT DESCRIPTION
SA14-123 MACHINE CONTROL INTERFACE ASSEMBLY
SA14-123 MACHINE CONTROL INTERFACE ASSEMBL Y
The resistor network on each opto-coupler input circuit, (R216 and R2oo on input #1 ) is selected so that an external 12 to 24 volt control voltage will operate the device
Address and Control
Address 7600H is the main select ine for the above discrete control inputs and outputs
In the ST series of Stereo Television consoles these control inputs are extended to a standard 6x26 Christmas tree block where 12 volts is made available.
Latch 418 holds the lower address bits which are derived off the main address/data bus The write select pulse for 418 is derived from address select 7600H and the ALE control Line.
If it becomes necessary to operate one of the control inputs from 5 volts then one of the isolating resistors must be shorted out (R216 via Link L4 for input #1) This may be achieved by remov ing the bottom cover from the MS2OOA and apply ing a small blob of solder to the shorting Tink. Ensure all power is off and grounding precautions are taken for operating on cMoS circuitry
The dual 1 of 4 decoder 419 serves as a read and write selector. A read function for the two data input buffers U21 and u22 and a write function for the two data output Latches .
Power_ Supply Decoupling
A polarity reversing diode is connected across each opto-coupler input, to prevent damage from the inadvertent application f a reverse controlling voltage_
The 5 volt supply. voltage is derived directly from the SA14-094 assembly through a breaker switch. Capacitive decoupling is pro- vided at the IC' s
U23 424 , U25 and 426 are each quad opto-couplers. The outputs from these devices feed into the 8 bit data buffers u21 and u22 The outputs of these data buffers feed back to the main SA14-094 assembly on the internal 8 bit address/data bus
The 10 volt supply voltage to the relays is decoupled by Q1 , Q2 , R251 C20 and C21
Discrete Data Outputs
Sixteen isolated control outputs at data port 7700H are available for controlling discrete functions_ Each output is a dry relay contact capable of swi itching 24V dc at 50 mA They are normally configured to an open contact for the resting state Optional Iy, by reversing the connections to connect Links Lz0 through to L35 a close contact may be achieved for the resting state This may be accomplished in software as wel | on a custom basis
The sixteen relays (K1 to K16) are driven from two 8 bit Latches u27 and u28 through level shifters U29, 430 and u31
A logic level stored in the latches will energize the appropriate relay_ The inputs to u27 and U28 are from the main internal 8 bit address/data bus. The Latch ou tputs are disabled at pin 1 of 427 and u28 , releasing the external control functions. This occurs when the re-triggerable one shot U20 does not get updated periodically which would occur if the operating system is not functioning correctly
1-2
1-3
```
